- **registry-audit**: Re-audit the SDLC registry — schema validation, orphan detection, overlap analysis, tech-agnosticism — with every finding adversarially verified ([AlexanderVegazo26/agents](https://github.com/AlexanderVegazo26/agents/blob/HEAD/commandcode-suite/commands/registry-audit.md) · 2 copies)

- **registry-list**: Read-only inspector for the project's resolution configuration. Touches no state — purely a window onto `vibe.toml ([vibevm/vibevm](https://github.com/vibevm/vibevm/blob/HEAD/docs-legacy/commands/registry-list.md) · ★11)
- **registry-manage**: Manage container registries (ECR, GCR, Harbor) ([jeremylongshore/tons-of-skills-marketplace](https://github.com/jeremylongshore/tons-of-skills-marketplace/blob/HEAD/plugins/devops/container-registry-manager/commands/registry-manage.md) · ★2.8k · 2 copies)
- **registry-pick**: Recomenda packs externos do registry que servem pro projeto atual. Roda duas vezes no wizard — packs de design no 3.1, stack completa no 4.3. Nunca instala nada. ([lglucas/ai-dev-operating-system](https://github.com/lglucas/ai-dev-operating-system/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/registry-pick.md) · ★12)
- **registry-publish**: Maintainer-side command. Takes a directory containing a `vibe.toml` with a `[package]` table and publishes it as a tagged release in the configured registry's organization. Creates the per-package repo (or reuses an existing one), pushes contents, tags the version. Mechanical only — no semantic / LLM-backed review (that's reserved for v2+ per [`VIBEVM-SPEC.md` §8.5](../../VIBEVM-SPEC.md)). ([vibevm/vibevm](https://github.com/vibevm/vibevm/blob/HEAD/docs-legacy/commands/registry-publish.md) · ★11)
- **registry-redirect**: Maintainer-side command. Creates a registry stub repo carrying `vibe-redirect.toml` instead of package content; consumers reach the package transparently through the stub via `vibe install &lt;pkgref&gt;`. Per PROP-002 §2.4.2; see [`docs/registry-redirect.md`](../registry-redirect.md) for the full operator reference. ([vibevm/vibevm](https://github.com/vibevm/vibevm/blob/HEAD/docs-legacy/commands/registry-redirect.md) · ★11)
- **registry-redirect-sync**: Maintainer-side command. Reads an existing registry stub's `vibe-redirect.toml`, enumerates target-side tags, and pushes the missing ones into the stub. Per PROP-002 §2.4.2 `pass-through-tag` policy: the stub's tags determine which target versions the org's namespace exposes; this command keeps them in sync. ([vibevm/vibevm](https://github.com/vibevm/vibevm/blob/HEAD/docs-legacy/commands/registry-redirect-sync.md) · ★11)
- **registry-redirect-update**: Maintainer-side command. Mutates the `vibe-redirect.toml` of an existing stub repo in-place: each flag is optional, so the command does a true partial update — fields not passed retain their current value. Closes the [v0 manual-procedure gap](../registry-redirect.md#manual-procedure-fallback) where retargeting a stub required a hand-driven `git clone` / edit / push. ([vibevm/vibevm](https://github.com/vibevm/vibevm/blob/HEAD/docs-legacy/commands/registry-redirect-update.md) · ★11)
- **registry-remove**: Mutates `vibe.toml` to remove either a registry block (by name) or a mirror block (by exact `(of, url)` match). The two targets are spelled as subsubcommands so the CLI surface is unambiguous and shell completion works cleanly: ([vibevm/vibevm](https://github.com/vibevm/vibevm/blob/HEAD/docs-legacy/commands/registry-remove.md) · ★11)
- **registry-set-mirror**: Mutates `vibe.toml` to add a new `[[mirror]]` block. A mirror is a transparent alternative URL for a registry: when the primary URL fails (or by priority order), the resolver falls through to the mirror with no change to the lockfile or to per-package identity. `vibe registry set-mirror` is the manifest-side configuration; runtime mirror dispatch lands in M1.6 Phase B. ([vibevm/vibevm](https://github.com/vibevm/vibevm/blob/HEAD/docs-legacy/commands/registry-set-mirror.md) · ★11)
- **registry-sync**: Walks the project's `vibe.lock` and refreshes the on-disk clone of every package referenced by it — the per-package model's equivalent of `git fetch` against a single monorepo. Useful before a `vibe install` of a new version when you want the freshest tag list, and as a cache-warming step in CI. ([vibevm/vibevm](https://github.com/vibevm/vibevm/blob/HEAD/docs-legacy/commands/registry-sync.md) · ★11)
- **registry-test**: Read-only diagnostic. For every `[[registry]]` configured in the project's `vibe.toml`, the command performs a single `git ls-remote` (with token injection if `auth = "token-env"`) and reports the registry's current operational status without cloning, downloading packages, or mutating any local state. ([vibevm/vibevm](https://github.com/vibevm/vibevm/blob/HEAD/docs-legacy/commands/registry-test.md) · ★11)
- **registry-vendor**: Walks the project's `vibe.lock` and produces a directory containing one bare git repo per `[[registry]]`-served lockfile entry. The directory is a drop-in source for `[[mirror]] url = "file:///&lt;abs-path&gt;"` so subsequent installs can resolve everything without network access (offline / air-gapped path). ([vibevm/vibevm](https://github.com/vibevm/vibevm/blob/HEAD/docs-legacy/commands/registry-vendor.md) · ★11)

([arbiterForge/codeArbiter](https://github.com/arbiterForge/codeArbiter/blob/HEAD/core/surface/commands/release.md) · ★145 · 2 copies)
